1319.TT 東陽 
 邁入傳統旺季 營運逐季回升

[財務資訊更新]   
   公司簡介 
公司主要營業項目為汽 、 機車零組件 ， 為台灣塑膠件供應商 ， 也是全球第一大汽車AM
塑膠件製造商。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 100 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】買進，100 
【大盤指數/股價】46128 / 82.6

➢ 2Q26 財報符合預期 
回顧 2Q26 營運概況，毛利率為 32.5%，符合原先預期的 32.4%。營業
費用為 9.5 億元，較預期的 9.78 億元低。營益率為 15.76%，優於預期
的 15.2%。EPS 則為 1.36 元，優於預期的 1.21 元，整體獲利率小幅優
於預期。2Q26 營收 56.63 億元，QoQ+0.57%，YoY-3.92%。EPS 1.36
元，QoQ-15.58%，YoY+76.7%。 
➢ 3Q26 關稅已下滑至 15% 客戶庫存回補逐漸顯現 
隨著關稅大幅自 27.5%下滑至 15%，使客戶對庫存回補需求逐漸顯現。
目前客戶之庫存水位約在一個月左右，而越靠近北半球之 AM 需求旺季
時，客戶有將庫存備至 2~3 個月之需求，時間點約落在 9~10 月之間，
因此產能利用率屆時將自當前的 90%提升至 95%左右之水準 ， 同事帶動
毛利率因經濟規模提升而成長 。2026 上半年之 AM 件月營收約落在 14.4
億元上下，鑒於邁入 2026 下半年營運旺季，期望 AM 件月營收能提升
至 15.5 億元左右之水準，當前 7 月之 AM 件營收已達 15.5 億元，後續
有望以此為低標向上成長 。OEM 方面 ， 台灣約佔32%， 中國則佔約68%，
台灣市場預計將微幅成長 3%，中國則估計衰退個位數百分比。 
總體而言，受惠 2026 下半年邁入傳統 AM 件旺季，營收有望呈現逐季
成長之態勢。研究員預估 3Q26 營收 58.98 億元， QoQ+4.15%，
YoY+9.19%。EPS 1.44 元，QoQ+5.27%，YoY+29.3%。 
➢ 股價回檔為長期買入點 評價偏低 維持買進建議  
考量 AM 件之整體市場規模每年仍持續成長，公司持續開發的模具數量
也成為高度的競爭障礙，估計短期難有取代公司的 AM 件碰撞廠商來進
行競爭。研究員預估 2026 年/2027 年營收 233.31 億元/249.15 億元，
YoY-7.03% / +6.79%，EPS 6.01 元/6.29 元，YoY-6.53% / +4.65%。
當前評價約位於 12 倍，位於過往區間12X~23X 的中下緣位置，當前預
估殖利率約 5.6%，具下檔殖利率保護，維持買進投資建議，目標價100
元(2027F PE 16X)。

個股報告

公司介紹 
東陽成立於 1967 年，主要營業項目為汽機車零組件，為台灣塑膠件供應商，也是全
球第一大汽車 AM 塑膠件製造商，AM 塑膠件市占率約達 70%，而 AM 鈑金件全球
市佔率約 38%。除了在台南本廠有生產基地外，在桃園、中國大陸、美國、義大利等
地共設有 25 座生產據點。公司產品項目多元，包括引擎蓋、儀表板、車內飾板、行
李箱蓋、擾流板、前後保險桿、水箱護罩、水箱支架等塑料/鈑金件，產品多元使公司
可提供全球 85%~90%的碰撞零組件，約當超過 25,000 個品項，且每年持續投入約
18 億元之模具開發費用。產品營收佔比方面，AM 營收佔比約 75%、OEM 營收佔比
約 25%。以 AM 產品組合畫分來看，其中以保險桿為大宗，約佔 40%，水箱護罩、
水箱支架、葉子鈑、引擎蓋等分別佔 14%、3%、10%、11%。OEM 產品組合則以保
險桿為主，約佔 31%，儀錶板、水箱護罩、模具分別佔 17%、3%、7%。 
圖 1：東陽 AM 件產品項目

資料來源：東陽公司資料 
2Q26 財報符合預期 
回顧 2Q26 營運概況，毛利率為 32.5%，符合原先預期的 32.4%。營業費用為 9.5 億
元，較預期的 9.78 億元低。營益率為 15.76%，優於預期的 15.2%。EPS 則為 1.36
元，優於預期的 1.21 元，整體獲利率小幅優於預期。關稅在非半導體 232 關稅優惠
措施實施前，客戶拉貨仍面臨 27.5%之關稅，使庫存回補力道減弱，而 2Q 本來就為
該產業的營運淡季，庫存回補需求持續偏弱，導致 4 月、5 月、6 月營收持續呈現月
減之狀態 ， 預估2Q26 為全年營運谷底 ， 後續獲利將穩步向上回升 。2Q26 營收 56.63
億元，QoQ+0.57%，YoY-3.92%。EPS 1.36 元，QoQ-15.58%，YoY+76.7%。 
3Q26 關稅已下滑至 15% 客戶庫存回補逐漸顯現 
隨著關稅大幅自 27.5%下滑至 15%， 使客戶對庫存回補需求逐漸顯現 。 目前客戶之庫
存水位約在一個月左右， 而越靠近北半球之AM 需求旺季時 ，客戶有將庫存備至2~3
個月之需求，時間點約落在 9~10 月之間，因此產能利用率屆時將自當前的 90%提升
至 95%左右之水準，同事帶動毛利率因經濟規模提升而成長。2026 上半年之 AM 件
月營收約落在 14.4 億元上下，鑒於邁入 2026 下半年營運旺季，期望 AM 件月營收
能提升至 15.5 億元左右之水準，當前 7 月之 AM 件營收已達 15.5 億元，後續有望以
此為低標向上成長。OEM 方面，台灣約佔 32%，中國則佔約 68%，台灣市場預計將

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
微幅成長 3%， 中國則估計衰退個位數百分比 。 總體而言 ， 受惠2026 下半年邁入傳統
AM 件旺季，營收有望呈現逐季成長之態勢。研究員預估 3Q26 營收 58.98 億元，
QoQ+4.15%，YoY+9.19%。EPS 1.44 元，QoQ+5.27%，YoY+29.3%。 
長期 AM 件仍將逐漸轉往 OEM 件 使東陽可觸及市場穩步擴大 
展望未來，AM 市場持續在侵蝕 OEM 市場，以台灣而言，碰撞更換零組件市場中以
AM 為大宗，約佔 80%，OEM 則約佔 20%。美國使用 AM 件的佔比也從過去的個位
數%提升到目前的 21%，估計在 2030 年可達到 28%左右之水準。歐洲則從過去的
20+%提升到 30%。主因歐美地區的保險公司為了降低支出成本，持續鼓勵客戶選擇
使用品質與 OEM 件相近的 AM 件，一方面保險公司可以降低支出成本，另一方面被
保險人可以降低他的保費支出，達成雙贏的局面，也是AM 件在全球的佔比能持續穩
步提升的主要原因 。 因此在AM 件滲透率持續提升的狀況下 ， 公司每年的AM 件營收
都能保有 5%~8%的穩定成長，屬於穩健成長的經營型態。 
在競爭優勢方面，公司模具數量多，可提供超過 25,000 個品項，涵蓋了全球 80%在
路上跑的車型，且模具開發成本高、開發時間長(平均需約 6~8 個月)，東陽每年持續
開發 300~400 套模具，形成了高度的進入門檻。使AM 件的市佔率能維持在 70%以
上的高市佔率表現 。 除此之外 ， 保險公司理賠的AM 件還必須通過 CAPA 認證 ，CAPA
為美國獨立認證組織(Certified Automotive Parts Association) ，通過認證的零件表
示其品質與 OEM 件相同或是更高，在歐美市場更受到保險公司(如 State Farm)及消
費者的信任。 
股價回檔為長期買入點 評價偏低 維持買進建議  
考量 AM 件之整體市場規模每年仍持續成長 ， 公司持續開發的模具數量也成為高度的
競爭障礙，估計短期難有取代公司的 AM 件碰撞廠商來進行競爭。研究員預估 2026
年/2027 年營收 233.31 億元/249.15 億元，YoY-7.03% / +6.79%，EPS 6.01 元/6.29
元，YoY-6.53%/+4.65%。當前評價約位於 12 倍， 位於過往區間12X~23X 的中下緣
位置，當前預估殖利率約 5.6%，具下檔殖利率保護，維持買進投資建議，目標價100
元(2027F PE 16X)。

2317 鴻海精密工業(FOXCONN)

報告日期： 2026/09/03         研究員：曾智楷 (Kevin Tseng) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一、 2Q26 營收、獲利續創同期新高， EPS 4.27 元 
鴻海 2Q26 營收 2.53 兆， QoQ +19% ，YoY +41% ，創歷年同期
新高。就各產別來看， 2Q26 雲端網路產品佔整體營收的 51% ；
消費智能產品佔 29% ；電腦終端產品佔 15% ；元件及其他產品佔
5%，由於第二季為 ICT 產品的傳統淡季， AI 伺服器營收持續走
高，以至於雲端網路產品比重首次突破五成。而獲利方面， 2Q26
營業毛利 1545.3 億， QoQ +18% ，YoY +36% ，毛利率 6.12% ，
QoQ -0.06ppts ，YoY -0.21ppts ，營業利益 948 億， QoQ +25% ，
YoY+68% ，營利率 3.75% ，QoQ +0.18ppts ，YoY +0.6ppts ，創
歷年單季最高。 2Q26 業外淨利 0.63 億，稅後淨利 599.7 億，
QoQ +20% ，YoY +35% ，單季稅後 EPS 4.27 元，創同期新高。

二、進入下半年傳統旺季、 AI 動能續強， 3Q26 營運有
望持續成長  
公司公告 7 月營收 9465 億， MoM +15.2% ，YoY +54.2% ，今年
累計營收 5.59 兆，年增 37.9% 。展望 3Q26 ，公司表示，受惠於
AI 動能持續成長，再加上 ICT 產品進入下半年的傳統旺季，預期
第三季營收將顯著成長。而就各產品別來看，雲端網路產品方面，
受惠於 AI 需求維持高速成長，且 CSP 客戶持續上調資本支出，
帶動 AI 機櫃持續放量，公司預估季對季與年對年都將呈現強勁成
長，並預估第三季 AI 機櫃出貨量將較第二季呈現高雙位數成長。
消費智能產品部分，則由於進入傳統旺季，預期季對季與年對年
皆將有顯著成長。而電腦終端產品方面，由於新產品需求高峰已
過，再加上記憶體供應緊張，預期季對季將呈現略微衰退。預估
公司 3Q26 營收 2.83 兆， QoQ +12.8% ，YoY +37.7% ，毛利率
6.1% ，營利率 3.6% ，稅後淨利 683 億元，單季稅後 EPS 4.92
元。

三、預估 2026 年 EPS 18.63 元 
展望 2026 年，由於 AI 伺服器需求維持強勁需求，訂單能見度提
升，公司維持全年強勁成長的看法不變。 AI 機櫃部分，公司維持
全年 AI 機櫃出貨量倍數以上成長預期，並預計 GB 系列產品需求
將至少延續至明年，整體出貨逐季成長的預期不變。而針對新一
代 AI 機櫃，公司預計第三季開始進行量產準備，第四季開始出貨，
預期在新一代平台的市佔率將進一步提升。 LPU 及 Vera 相關產

品目前處於前期量產準備階段，預計將在第三季開始生產，第四
季逐步放量。而網通產品部分， 800G 以上交換機全年營收有望
倍增， CPO 全光交換機也將按計畫在第三季量產出貨。另外，

估今年全年將超越去年 3.2% 的表現。至於資本支出方面，為因應
AI 伺服器機櫃及區域製造需求，預期全年資本支出仍將年增 3 成
以上，用於擴充伺服器、機櫃等關鍵產能，並強化全球製造與自

6.1% ，營利率 3.6% ，稅後淨利 2588 億，全年稅後 EPS 18.63
元。預估 2027 全年營收 12.4 兆， YoY +15% ，毛利率 5.9% ，營
利率 3.3% ，稅後淨利 3116 億，全年稅後 EPS 22.43 元。

四、投資建議為「買進」，目標價 335 元 
考量到 CSP 持續上修 2026 年資本支出展望， AI 伺服器維持強勁
需求，公司預期今年 AI 機櫃出貨量將達成倍數成長，且隨著新一
代 AI 機櫃推出，有望進一步拓展公司在 AI 伺服器領域市佔率。
目前公司股價交易於 2027 年 EPS 約 11~12 倍本益比，評價仍有
提升空間，給予投資建議為買進，目標價 335 元(15x 2027 EPS) 。

貳、公 司 概 況

一. 公司簡介  
鴻海精密工業 (FOXCONN) 為全球最大 eCMMS( 電子化 -零元件、
模組機光電垂直整合服務 )廠，在電子代工服務領域 (EMS) 排名全
球第一，市占率超過四成。鴻海近年積極投入「電動車、數位健
康、機器人」三大新興產業以及「人工智慧、半導體、新世代通
訊技術」三項新技術領域，以「 3+3 」結合作為集團重要的長期
發展策略。在 EMS 板塊，鴻海主要競爭對手為和碩聯合科技、
廣達電腦、緯創資通、仁寶電腦、英業達及立訊精密等。

圖一、鴻海產品營收佔比

資料來源：鴻海、宏遠投顧整理

1. 四大 CSP 上修今年資本支出達 7,450 億美元  
在生成式 AI 商機快速擴張帶動下，全球雲端基礎設施投資進入新
一輪高峰。亞馬遜、 Alphabet 和微軟認為其在先前的鉅額投資已
帶動營收明顯成長，同時預估未來 AI 潛在商機仍持續擴大。其中，
亞馬遜、 Alphabet 和 Meta 已陸續上調 2026 全年投資金額，且
四大雲端服務供應商 (CSP) 總資本支出將上升至 7,450 億美元新
高規模。亞馬遜、微軟、 Alphabet 與 Meta 2026 年第 1、2 季分
別為 1,306 億、 1,701 億美元，同時亞馬遜、 Alphabet 和 Meta 再
上修 2026 全年資本支出金額， 2026 年資本支出預估將達 7,450
億美元。而根據 TrendForce 最新產業研究，全球主要雲端服務
供應商 (CSP) 為加速建置 AI 基礎設施，資本支出預估於 2026 年
將年增 98% ，2027 年再成長 50% 至 1.3 兆美元左右，年增率收
斂的主要原因，除了反映高基期因素之外，部分可歸咎為鉅額資

本支出對四大 CSP 財務衝擊日益擴大。然而，在 AI Inference 、
Agentic AI 、自研 ASIC 和新一代 AI 模型持續推升算力需求的情
況下，預期整體 CSP 投資金額將續創新高紀錄，產業成長有望從
原先以 GPU 擴張為主，轉為包含 AI server 、液冷散熱、先進封
裝、高速互聯、電源和記憶體等基礎設施全面升級的態勢。

圖二、 2024-2027 年全球九大 CSP 資本支出總額預估 (單位 :十億美元 )

資料來源： TrendForce 、宏遠投顧整理

2. 2026 年全球 AI 伺服器出貨量預計年增 31% 
根據 TrendForce 最新 AI server 產業研究，近期超大型 CSP 、
Tier-2 資料中心業者對 NVIDIA GB/VR 等整櫃式 AI server 方案的
採購意願明顯提高，且 Google 、AWS 新一代 ASIC 平台於 2026
下半年陸續放量，加上中系業者擴大採用本地 AI 方案以滿足雲端
大型語言模型 (LLM) AI 需求，促使 TrendForce 上調 2026 年 AI 
server 出貨年成長幅度，從原先的 28% 更新為近 31% 。北美五大
業者 2026 年的資本支出多翻倍提升，反映出大型 CSP 廠持續擴
建 AI 資料中心、 GPU 叢集、液冷散熱等新一代基礎設施，以應
對生成式 AI 及大型模型快速成長帶來的算力需求。其中，
Google 2026 至 2027 年都將側重自家 TPU 晶片發展， 2027 年出
貨量有望繼續高速成長。 AWS 2026 年以 NVIDIA GB300 作為
GPU AI server 主力，自研 ASIC 也穩定出貨，預期至 2027 年量
能將再擴大。 Meta 2026 年主要採用 NVIDIA GB/VR 、 AMD 
Helios 整櫃式方案，自研 ASIC 布局預計至 2027 年將明顯加速，
為該公司長期降低 AI 基礎設施成本、提升推論效率的重要策略。

志聖 (2467 TT) 
設備含量進入高速成長期

永豐觀點 
志聖作為一家以熱處理技術為核心的製程設備平台，隨 AI 封裝與 PCB 朝向更大、
更厚、更多層發展，單位產能設備使用次數、站點，以及設備價值都將增加，使設
備含量(Equipment Content)成長快於客戶產能擴張速度，我們認為這是志聖有別
於單純設備循環股的核心差異化邏輯。 
 投資評價與建議 
初次評等給予買進建議，目標價 755 元：(1) 台積電 CoWoS/SoIC 2022 -2027 
CAGR 分別達>80%/>90%，且封裝尺寸放大、站點增加帶動單位產能設備密集度
提升，以 2.5D 封裝為例，平均每 2-3kwpm 的產能擴充可帶動志聖 250-350 萬
美元營收商機，成長力道高於客戶產能增幅，後續有望由面板級封裝接棒，挹注長
期動能，(2) AI Server 帶動 HDI/HLC PCB 與 ABF 載板 2026-2027 年接力放量，
分散先進封裝前兩大客戶集中度風險，(3) 產品組合持續往半導體、先進 PCB 轉
進，帶動毛利率由 49.1%升至 50.1%，2027 年稅後淨利成長(+51.1%)優於營收成
長(+30.6%)。 我們預估2026/2027 年 EPS 為 16.94/25.15 元， 並給予目標價755
元(30x 2027 EPSF)。

### Page 2

2

志聖以光與熱為核心，發展熱處理平台：志聖成立於 1966 年 ， 目前資本額15.7 億
新台幣，為 G2C 集團當中的核心成員之一，以紫外光、烘烤、壓膜塗佈、電漿製
程等技術提供相關壓膜、貼、撕膜、烘烤、電鍍相關設備。公司初期以 PCB 設備
起家，而後伴隨台灣電子產業升級，切入半導體、組裝、面板、太陽能等產業，近
年陸續在 IC 載板、高階HDI、先進封裝、HBM 等高附加價值市場發光發熱。公司
也與均豪、均華、東捷組成G2C+聯盟，共同實現台灣先進封裝在地化。志聖主要
生產據點位於台灣林口、台中、中國廣州、昆山，並在台南、廣東、泰國、美國鳳
凰城有服務據點。2025 年營收以產業別劃分，半導體佔約 40%、先進 PCB 27%、
傳統 PCB 24%、其他電子 6%、FPC 4%。主要競爭對手包含 EVG、TOK、SUSS、
Yield Engineering Systems (YES) 等。客戶包含台積電、日月光、臻鼎、力成、
Amkor、Micron、欣興、勝宏等電子中上游廠商。 
熱處理平台為公司核心，而非單純設備供應商：市場普遍將志聖視為烘箱(Oven)的
設備商，但我們認為其競爭力建立在對熱處理的知識上。在先進封裝中， 「加熱」
不只是單純提高溫度，而是透過精準控制升溫的速率、保溫時間、壓力，以及冷卻
曲線，使材料完成交聯反應(Cross-linking)，降低材料內部殘留應力，避免受熱物
體產生孔洞(Void)、翹曲(Warpage)等影響可靠度的問題。例如貼合(Lamination)
設備需要控制樹脂的流動與貼合的均勻性；壓力烘箱 (Pressure oven)則必須同時
控制溫度與壓力，使填充材料(Underfill)在低黏度狀態下充分流動並完成固化；電
漿設備則透過表面活化 ， 提高材料附著力並降低後續脫層(De-lamination)的風險 。
雖然設備種類不同，但背後都建立在對材料、熱、壓力與應力控制能力上，這也是
公司能從過往 PCB 延伸至 IC 載板，再跨入 CoWoS、HBM 與未來面板級封裝的
原因。 
我們將志聖的主要產品區分以應用類型劃分為貼(貼合)、壓(壓合)、撕(撕膜)、烤(熱
處理)、表(表面處理)五大類，如表一所示。

營運現況與分析 
圖一：志聖營收組合-產業別已由過往 PCB 向上延伸至半導體先進封裝

資料來源：志聖；永豐投顧研究部整理，Aug. 2026

### Page 3

3

貼-「貼合」對應公司的 Lamination 設備，主要用於將薄膜、膠材均勻地貼附在
PCB/載板/矽晶圓/面板等載體上，而常見的材料包含 ABF 絕緣膜、乾式光阻、晶
粒黏著膜、非導電薄膜(NCF)、保護膜及暫時接合膠。對於 IC 載板製程而言，ABF
膜貼合是增層(build up)過程的核心步驟，每一層線路製作，都需要貼膜、固化、
雷射鑽孔、除膠渣、鍍銅等流程，逐步堆疊成載板結構。藉由志聖的貼膜 /真空貼
膜設備，可協助客戶提升貼合的均勻性、降低氣泡與污染，並確保後續細線路製程
的良率。 
壓-「壓合」是公司在先進封裝價值提升最明顯的能力之一 ， 對應著公司的Bonder、
除泡和抗翹曲設備。(1) 在先進封裝中，暫時性鍵合與解鍵合(TBDB)已成為不可或
缺的技術之一。 為避免晶圓在減薄過程中因機械強度不足而破損， 須先在承載晶圓
/元件晶圓上形成暫時接合膠層，而後利用志聖的 Bonder 進行接合。面板級封裝
則需要處理更大尺寸的方形載具 ，而志聖過去累積許多面板設備經驗， 同時處理過
圓形矽晶圓和方形的面板，因此在大尺寸的貼合與搬運上具有一定的競爭力。 (2) 
抗翹曲設備的重要性正與日俱增 ，係因隨著先進封裝朝向更大尺寸發展， 封裝內部
不同材料 CTE 差異，在加熱過程當中容易產生翹曲。而當封裝體的平整度不足，
可能會影響後續對晶片對位、接合品質、底部填充材料的流動均勻性，甚至影響最
終組裝良率，因此，需要抗翹曲設備來重新校正封裝平整度，協助客戶提升後續製
程穩定性。

### Page 4

4

另外在 G2C+集團中，志聖與均華雖然都有 Bonder，但兩者角色不同。志聖主要
提供 wafer／carrier bonding ，均華則偏向 die bonding 與 die attach ；公司過
去也將自身定位於 CoW 轉 oS 過程中的 temporary bond 設備供應商。而在壓力
烤箱的部分 ， 志聖相較於印能則屬於後進者 ， 目前主要出貨給台灣中部的OSAT 廠
商。 
撕-「剝膜」能力主要對應公司的 Peeler 設備 ， 最典型的應用是IC 載板與高階 PCB。
由於 ABF 膜在出廠時，通常會包含上層的保護膜(release film) 及下層可能會有承
載膜(Carrier film) ，而這 1~2 層膜在與基板貼合時需進行移除，惟剝膜的速度、
角度、張力、靜電與殘膠都有可能會影響產品良率。舉例來說，如速度過快，容易
產生靜電與粒子吸附 ； 若速度過慢 ， 則可能留有殘膠 ， 或是影響產能(Throughput)。
若張力不均，也可能造成膜材斷裂、基板損傷，因此高階的 Peeler 更為廠商所需
要。 
烤-「熱處理」是公司最基礎 、 應用範圍最廣的核心能力 ， 主要產品包含Auto Oven、
Cure Oven 等產品，以應用來看，(1) 在傳統 PCB 領域，烤箱主要用於清洗或鑽
孔後的去除水氣、烘乾板材，以及固化樹脂，之所以重要，係因若未充分烘乾，可
能影響後續壓合、鍍銅時的接著力與可靠度，隨著 PCB 朝向更厚、更大，以及多
層數發展，烘烤的時長(影響含水量)與均溫控制難度同步增加，烘烤站點本身的重
要性也在提升。(2) 在 IC 載板領域，主用於 ABF 膜與核心板材貼合後的固化，以
形成穩定的介電層。隨著增層數量增加，每一層都需要重複貼膜、固化、鑽孔與鍍
銅，因此載板層數越多，對烤箱/熱處理設備的需求也越高。(3) 在先進封裝領域，
志聖的烤箱產品進一步延伸到底部填充膠材的固化、TBDB 時的烘烤，使膠材由流
動狀態轉變為具有機械強度的固態材料。在 Underfill 填充與固化過程中，填充膠
材需要透過流入 µbump 之間的狹小空間，若流動不均或是空氣無法排除，就可能
形成氣泡， 而透過壓力烤箱則可同時利用熱與壓力改善封裝過程中形成的氣泡。綜
上所述 ， 我們認為公司在熱處理的競爭力 ， 不只是製造烤箱 ， 而是控制升降溫速率 、
箱內均溫性、大尺寸基板的熱流分佈等。 
表-「表面處理」對應到公司的電漿及 UV 固化設備，在 PCB、IC 載板和先進封裝
中，包含著清洗、除膠、表面活化及提升材料附著力的關鍵步驟，儘管這些製程並
不直接影響線路或是封裝結構 ， 但卻是確保後續鍍銅、 貼合及封裝可靠度的重要過
圖三：Peeler 使用站點與目的

資料來源：公開資訊；永豐投顧研究部整理，Aug. 2026

### Page 5

5

程。(1) 在 PCB/IC 載板製程中，雷射鑽孔後，孔洞側壁通常會有材料樹脂殘渣
(Smear)， 如果沒有完全去除， 將影響後續鍍銅的均勻性 ，同時降低導通的可靠度 。
因此需透過除膠渣及表面清洗製程，去除殘留的樹脂，以提升後續鍍銅的附著力。
(2) 在先進封裝中則有許多製程都需要潔淨且活化的表面材料 ， 如晶片接合面 、RDL
製程等，以提升材料濕潤性和接著力，降低氣泡與分層(De-lamination)的風險，
進而提高封裝可靠度。(3) 此外，志聖也將相關技術延伸至再生晶圓廠，用於將晶
圓表面污染物的去除，協助客戶提升再生晶圓的品質。 
產業概況-四大資本支出主線同步展開：截至 1H26，半導體和 PCB 產業合計占志
聖營收約 92%，而公司主要客戶的擴產態度日益積極。我們將志聖的成長分類成
「量增」與「質變」兩條主軸， 「量增」來自上游製造廠商全面擴產； 「質變」則來
自封裝尺寸放大、ABF 和 PCB 層數提高、翹曲控制需求增加、自動化程度更多，
以及製程站點的增加，兩者都將帶動客戶單位產能對設備的密集度或複雜度提升，
進而使志聖營收成長 。 需求來源則分為四條主線 ， 包括(1) 台積電 CoWoS/SoIC 持
續擴產，推升半導體設備需求；(2) 日月光、矽品與力成等 OSAT 加速先進封裝投
資，擴大志聖半導體設備可服務市場；(3) AI PCB/HDI/HLC 進入新一輪擴產週期，
成為 PCB 設備第二成長曲線 ；(4) ABF 載板自 2026 年重新啟動資本支出 ， 為2027
年增添額外成長動能。 
台積電先進封裝擴產，志聖設備 Content同步提升：台積電的 2026 年資本支出
達 600-640 億美元，其中先進封裝、測試、光罩及其他項目仍占 10-20%，顯示
先進封裝仍維持高強度投資。除產能擴張外，隨著CoWoS 封裝尺寸由 2024 年約
3.3x 光罩尺寸，逐步朝2029 年 14x 以上發展、AI 系統功耗也由約 1400w 繼續提
升，單位產能所需熱處理、烘烤，以及自動化設備也將同步提升。研究部預期每新
增 2-3kwpm CoWoS/CoWoS-Like 產能，可帶動志聖約 250-350 萬美元的設備
商機，而現階段我們預估台積電 CoWoS 月產能將由 4Q26 的 140kwpm 提升至
4Q27 的 190kwpm，並在 2028 年達到 220kwpm 後將重心轉往 SoIC/CoPoS 產
能建置，目前 CoWoS 的既有供應商多已在 2-3Q26 將設備陸續送往龍潭廠，協
助客戶進行技術研發，我們認為將在 2028 年起帶動台灣設備廠下波成長動能。另
一方面，台積電 2025 年台灣的在地採購零組件比例已達到 45.3%，高於原定的
45%目標，預期在 2026 年達到 48%，並持續朝 2030 年的 60%邁進，使得志聖
除受惠先進封裝資本支出成長外 ，也具備國產化滲透率提升的機會， 因此台積電仍
為公司 2026-2027 年半導體設備最主要的動能之一。 
圖四：產業轉型，志聖受惠

資料來源：公開資；永豐投顧研究部整理，Aug. 2026

### Page 6

6

OSAT買廠以加速佈局： 除台積電以外 ，OSAT 端亦進入新一輪先進封裝擴產週期 。
日月光、矽品、力成等OSAT 近年積極收購既有廠房並改建無塵室，可縮短廠房取
得至設備移入的時程，因此 2026 年買廠與廠務工程可視為 2H26-2027 年設備需
求的領先指標；除自建廠房外，日月光/矽品亦公告多筆廠務工程訊息，目前最遠
能見度至 2028 年底(亦即規劃 2029 年產能)，同樣顯示此次封測擴產並非短期規
劃，而是長期佈局。 
另一方面，日月光已將 2027 年 LEAP 營收目標提高到至少 75 億美元，為 2026
年的兩倍以上 ， 並同步提高資本支出 ， 顯示OSAT 的投資重心正由傳統封測轉向先
進封裝，包括 oS 段、FOCoS 及 Turnkey 服務。研究部預估日月光的 LEAP 產能
將由 4Q26 的 25kwpm 提升至 4Q27 的 45kwpm，也因 OSAT 對設備自動化有
更高的需求，有助公司增加單一設備產值，並降低對單一晶圓代工客戶的依賴。雖
然公司先進封裝前兩大客戶目前仍佔相關營收 90%，但兩大客戶均持續進行中長
期佈局，帶動志聖設備交期由 4-10 個月進一步拉長，亦有助提升 2026-2027 及
中期需求能見度。 
對志聖而言，CoWoS、FOCoS 等先進封裝擴產除直接帶動設備數量需求外，製程
複雜度提升亦有助增加單位產能的設備需求。 先進封裝大量使用高分子材料、薄
圖五：晶片結構日益複雜

資料來源：台積電；永豐投顧研究部預估及整理，Aug. 2026

### Page 7

7

膜、底部填充膠與封模材料，RDL、載板及封裝結構製程中需經過多次烘烤、真空
除泡、貼合及固化，以降低氣泡、翹曲及黏著不良等問題。因此，隨CoWoS 產能擴
充及封裝結構日益複雜，志聖相關熱製程、真空除泡及貼合設備需求可望同步成長。 
PCB層數 x製程次數=志聖受惠潛力：隨 AI 伺服器、GPU/ASIC 及 800G/1.6T 交
換器需求成長，高階 PCB 持續朝高層數、細線路與 HDI 化發展，包含主流板層數
由 2022-2024 年的 16-26 層，朝 2026-2027 年的 28-36 層，甚至 44 層以上提
升，iHDI/RPCB 導入亦提高對位、均勻性與製程控制要求。由於 PCB 層數增加需
經過更多次壓膜、烘烤、對位及線路製作等製程，即使新增產能面積相同，單位產
能所需設備 Content 亦隨製程次數增加而提升。對志聖而言，受惠不僅來自 PCB
產能擴充，更來自高階化帶動設備使用強度增加。目前臻鼎、華通、健鼎、滬電、
勝宏及景旺均積極擴充 AI Server 用 HDI/HLC 產能，部分新產能於 2026 年進入
裝機、2027 年陸續放量，有望成為公司 2026–2027 年 PCB 設備營收的重要成長
動能。我們認為，本輪 PCB Capex 同時受惠「產能擴充」與「設備 Content 提
升」 ，志聖設備營收成長彈性有望優於PCB 產能增幅。 
2026年起 ABF廠重啟資本支出，挹注志聖 2027年另一動能：隨 AI GPU、ASIC
及高階 CPU 需求成長 ， 推升了高階載板規格升級 ， 包含尺寸由過往的31x31mm、
75x60mm 進一步朝 150x150mm 以上發展；層數由 6L、20L 提升至 28L 以上；
凸塊密度也逐步增加到 30-50 萬級。尺寸放大與層數增加，使 ABF 壓合、乾膜貼
附、線路製作及保護膜移除等製程次數與控制難度同步提高，國內廠商如欣興、南
電與景碩也在增加高階 ABF 載板投資。對志聖而言，除受惠 ABF 產能擴充外，亦
可受惠單位載板設備使用次數與製程精度要求提升，有望支撐 2026–2027 年相關
設備出貨成長。

圖六：志聖在先進封裝製程中的參與站點

資料來源：志聖；永豐投顧研究部整理，Aug. 2026

### Page 8

8

財務概況： 
綜上所述，我們預期志聖 2026–2027 年營運將同步受惠半導體與高階 PCB 設備
需求擴張。台積電 CoWoS 持續擴產仍為 2026-2027 年主要動能，而 SoIC 對於
產能擴充的需求在 2027 年更為強勁，2028 年後則有望由 CoPoS 接棒；日月光、
矽品等封測公司積極購廠並進行無塵室改建，目前至少有8 座新建廠房及 13 區自
建廠房，預期相關投資將逐步轉化為 2H26–2029 年設備需求；此外，AI Server 
帶動 HDI/HLC PCB 擴產，加上ABF 載板重啟資本支出，亦將增添後續成長動能。 
不同於過去需求主要來自產能擴充， 本輪亦受惠製程複雜度提升， 帶動單位產能設
備需求增加。隨 2.5D 封裝尺寸放大，氣泡、翹曲及材料應力控制難度提高；PCB 
與 ABF 則隨層數及 build-up 次數增加，使貼膜、固化與烘烤等製程次數提升，因
此志聖受惠不僅來自客戶 Capex 量增，亦來自製程複雜度提升，帶動單位產能設
備的使用次數、站點，以及設備價值量增加。 
研究部預估志聖 2026 年營收 115.02 億元(+88.5% YoY)，毛利率 49.1%，稅後淨
利 26.8 億元(+223.6%YoY)，EPS 16.94 元。展望 2027 年，隨 OSAT 進入設備大
量移入階段，有望帶動志聖獲利轉佳，預估2027 年營收 150 億元(+30.6%YoY)，
毛利率升至 50.1%，稅後淨利 40.6 億元(+51.1% YoY)，EPS 25.15 元。 
圖七：ABF 載板製造流程與志聖可能切入站點

### Page 2

Asia Pacific Semiconductors and Electronic Components &
Equipment
27 August 2026 Citi Research
2
•Custom ASIC Competition –   NVIDIA framed the ASIC competition around the economics and flexibility of the entire
“AI factory.” NVIDIA’s competitive advantage increasingly comes from integrating GPU compute, CPU, networking,
rack-scale architecture and software into one platform that can support multiple models and workloads across
different clouds. This changes the competitive discussion from whether a custom ASIC can outperform a GPU on a

specific task to whether hyperscalers can reproduce the same flexibility, software ecosystem and infrastructure
efficiency at scale. In our view, custom ASICs remain a credible competitive threat, particularly for stable and highly
optimized inference workloads, but NVIDIA's broader platform approach can defend its position even as the
accelerator market becomes. We would expect NVLink fusion more ready with AWS first in Trainium 4 generation, we

see Alchip would be the beneficiary as the major partner. We also see that Mediatek's early engagement with Nvidia
could be helpful for the company to generate more opportunity for second customer in ASIC in the longer term.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

•MediaTek becomes a much more credible AI ASIC contender . MediaTek 
already expects its first major AI accelerator ASIC to enter production in

second customer's ASIC wins as NVLink Fusion changes the competitive 
proposition: instead of MediaTek selling customers only an independently 
designed ASIC, it can potentially offer a custom XPU already engineered to 
coexist with NVIDIA GPUs, CPUs, networking and rack-scale NVLink 
infrastructure. According to NVIDIA, the cooperation will be deepening 
including : NVIDIA NVLink Fusion chiplet, connecting XPUs to the NVIDIA 
NVLink scale-up fabric using NVIDIA photonics or electrical 
interconnects;  NVIDIA NVLink-C2C, providing high-bandwidth, energy-
efficient connectivity between XPUs, NVIDIA Rosa CPUs and other 
compatible processors. NVIDIA NVHBM, integrating customized memory 
capabilities to increase bandwidth and energy efficiency while dedicating 
more silicon area to compute.

collaborate across multiple generations of both RTX Spark and DGX Spark products.   MediaTek therefore gains three
separate AI growth vectors: (1) hyperscaler custom AI ASICs, (2) NVIDIA-based AI PCs/local AI systems, and (3)
automotive/physical AI. Strategically, this is important because MediaTek can reuse the same core capabilities—
leading-edge CPU/SoC design, SerDes/interconnect, advanced packaging, connectivity and power optimization—

across all three markets. The result should be a progressively higher mix of large, advanced-node chips with much
greater dollar content than traditional smartphone SoCs, reducing MediaTek’s dependence on handset cycles.
•We view today’s announcement as structurally positive for MediaTek. The US$3.5bn investment validates the
partnership financially, it potentially moves MediaTek from being a supplier that happens to have a few large AI ASIC
wins toward becoming one of the principal custom-silicon design platforms inside the broader NVIDIA AI-factory

ecosystem. That could materially increase both the number and longevity of MediaTek’s AI ASIC opportunities in the
longer term.

### Page 3

Mediatek (2454.TW)
31 August 2026 Citi Research
3
Mediatek
Valuation
Our NT$6,800 target price (rounded) for MediaTek is based on 35x P/E to 2027/2028 EPS, which is 1.5-std above its five-year
average forward P/E given the promising outlook from its AI ASIC business starting from 2H26. We believe our target PE is also
justified by Mediatek's global and regional peers' trading at an average of 36x PER. AI ASIC development with CSPs should
provide more upside from 2027 onwards. We also see Mediatek well prepared for 6G and 6G+NTN Satellite communication.

Despite lukewarm smartphone overall deamand, we expect Mediatek to benefit from 5G migration in emerging markets and its
Dimensity series should also gain shares in flagship models. Non-smartphone business is also seeing stabilizing TV and IoT
demand with Mediatek outgrowing the overall market on its better technology in WiFi 7 and connectivity.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected growth from

AI ASIC business; 2) more severe competition in the smartphone segment; 3) slower overall demand related to macroeconomic
risks, especially for smartphone demand; and 4) further price hikes from foundries.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
MediaTek is a fabless IC design company supplying to 
wireless communications and digital consumer markets. 
Before taking leadership in mobile SoC area, it had built 
solid ground in the TV SoC and optical disk drive markets. 
The firm invests heavily in R&D to seek better positioning 
in the 5G cycle, and has a complete offering including ASIC, 
Wi-Fi, PMIC etc. to address applications beyond 
smartphone, such as IoT and auto.

Investment Rationale 
We have Buy rating on MediaTek as we think MediaTek is 
ready and has the strength to brace for the upcoming 
proliferation of IoT devices and opportunities in growing 
markets like ASIC, ARM-based PC, auto, and etc., and we 
believe its solid balance sheet and cashflow could provide 
downside support to the share price. Meanwhile, we expect 
5G smartphone penetration to be a multi-year theme for 
the company, with its better competitive landscape in 
duopoly 5G era and an improved margin profile.

Stock Data  
Price to Book Value 14.4x

### Page 3

Price objective basis & risk 
MediaTek (MDTKF) 
Our PO of NT$5,230 is based on 23x 2H27-1H28 P/E. as we are constructive on its 
broadening ASIC pipeline to drive earnings upgrade cycle and re-rating as it locks in 
more supply.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) better than expected smartphone demand, especially in 
China, (2) a better pricing in mobile communication segment thanks to alleviated 
competition and rising exposure to high-end/flagship market, and (3) an even more 
superior investment return on non-op side to uplift its net cash position. (4) Unexpected 
collaboration with global technology leaders leads to higher than expected 
margins/profits

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) weaker-than-expected end-market situation in 
consumer-related smartphone/TV space, (2) macro challenges including inflation/policy-
related pressures, (3) rising competition from existing players and/or newcomers in 
smartphone/TV SoC (system on chip) space, and (4) ASP/margin pressure caused by each 
or all of the above and/or rising foundry costs. (4) Bidding process for Meta Inference 
ASIC is slower than expected.

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating.  VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation). 
Investment rating Total return expectation (within 12-month period of date of initial 
rating)

secure) and 9 - pays no cash dividend.  Coverage Cluster is comprised of stocks covered by a single analyst or two or more analysts sharing a c ommon industry,

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
SEMICON Taiwan 2026
Takeaways
Key takeaways from CEO fireside chat, which included Hon Hai (HH) Chairman
Young Liu and Unimicron (UMTC) Chairman Shan-Chieh Chien:
• HH sees AI demand as  unprecedented in speed and scale, pressuring the
supply chain to expand capacity while raising capital intensity.
• Management views diversification as structural, accelerated by geopolitics
and countries' push for local production. We have written about this here,

here, and here.
• HH's strategy is "Made with Taiwan", exporting Taiwan's manufacturing IP,
system-integration know-how and execution speed to end markets via local
partnerships and production.
• UMTC said AI lifts ABF substrate demand and complexity (large sizes, higher
layer counts, more advanced designs), widening supply constraints. This
aligns with our view, which we have written here, here, and here.
• UMTC said it has held more than 10 meetings in the past 18 months with

suppliers and customers to align demand forecast and capacity, by actively
preparing capacity, preparing new facilities, securing land.
• Supply of upstream key materials, such as ABR and T-glass, remains tight.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

### Page 3

Hon Hai (2317.TW; Buy, on CL): Jul rev in line at +54% YoY; AI server and smartphones to support 3Q 
growth

Hon Hai’s Jul revenues were +54% YoY/ +15% MoM to NT$947m, or 2% higher than our 
estimates, overall in line. From a MoM perspective, Consumer Electronics & 
Components delivered significant growth MoM, benefitting from the pull-in 
momentum, followed by Cloud and Networking & Computing which achieved strong 
growth MoM. From a YoY perspective, all segments across Consumer Electronics, 
Components, Cloud and Networking & Computing delivered strong growth YoY, and 
management attributed this to the strong pull-in momentum and higher selling prices of 
Consumer Electronics and Computing products. We expect 3Q26E revenues to be +48% 
YoY / +22% QoQ to NT$3.1 trillion. June/ July delivered strong YoY (+52%/ 54% YoY), 
and we expect Aug revenues to remain strong at 73% YoY / 11% MoM to NT$1,051bn, 
supported by AI rack server ramp-up, and strong seasonality of the consumer 
electronics business. Maintain Buy (on CL).

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on a 18.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.

### Page 4

Quanta (2382.TW, Neutral): AI server in strong ramp up; PC to decline in 2H26 on high memory cost

Quanta’s Jul revenue was down 5% QoQ / up 131% YoY, or 21% higher than our 
estimate, which we attribute to the strong rack-level AI server shipments, along with the 
stronger than expected PC shipments at 4.5m units. Looking ahead, we expect 
sequential revenue decline in Aug-Sep, mainly on the memory price hike weighing on PC 
end demand. We model sequential revenue increases in Oct-Dec, supported by the next 
generation rack-level AI server shipments ramp up, which carries a higher ASP . Overall, 
we model Quanta’s 3Q / 4Q26E revenues to grow at 85% / 47% YoY, riding on the 
growing AI infrastructure trend. Maintain Neutral.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Quanta 
We are Neutral-rated on Quanta. Our 12-month target price of NT$299 is based on an 
11x 2027E P/E, which references PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple 
correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance.  
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery; 2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

### Page 6

AVC (3017.TW, Buy): Liquid cooling component ASP and GM upside on the rising complexity of server 
racks; July in line

AVC’s Jul revenue was up 57% YoY / 6% QoQ to NT$19bn, largely in line with our 
estimate. We attribute the strong YoY growth to the strong shipments of AI server liquid 
cooling components, riding on the increasing AI server end demand and a growing liquid 
cooling penetration rate. We model sequential revenue growth in Aug-Dec, considering 
(1) rising shipments of next-generation rack-level AI servers, (2) an increase in liquid 
cooling dollar content in next-generation AI server racks given their growing complexity, 
and (3) a rising liquid cooling penetration rate in both ASIC and GPU AI servers. Maintain 
Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - AVC 
We are Buy-rated on AVC. Our 12-month target price for AVC is based on a near-term 
P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our target price of 
NT$3,986 on a target P/E multiple of 28.2x applied to our forward-year EPS (2027E). 
Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers 
in technology hardware.  
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected adoption of liquid cooling; (2) 
lower-than-expected demand for AI servers; (3) fiercer-than-expected competition; 
and (4) a slower-than-expected recovery in general servers.

### Page 7

Wiwynn (6669.TW, Buy): July revenues sequentially increase; Capex strong increase shows strong AI 
demand

Wiwynn July revenues were in line with our estimates at NT$118bn, driven by solid 
general servers growth and its major ASIC customers’ new generation of ASIC AI servers 
(air-cooling) entering mass production since June 2026. Looking ahead, we model 
sequential revenues decline in Aug and Sep, considering the high-base of general 
servers, while liquid-cooled ASIC AI servers of its major customer, and GPU AI server 
racks ramp up is in 4Q26E. Nevertheless, the 3Q26 revenues growth would be much 
stronger than 1Q26 (-5% QoQ), and 2Q26 (+1% QoQ), up to +22% QoQ, and we expect 
further acceleration to +32% QoQ in 4Q26, driven by AI servers new models ramp up, AI 
servers expansion from ASIC to GPU, potential ASIC AI servers SKU expansion, and less 
impact of business model transition from Buy & Sell to Consign for general servers. 
Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wiwynn 
We are Buy-rated on Wiwynn with a 12-month TP of NT$3,352.55. Our target price is 
based on 20x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. 
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected AI server demand growth; 2) 
slower-than-expected recovery in general server demand; and 3) more severe 
competition in the server ODM market.

### Page 8

Wistron (3231.TW, Buy): July and 2Q26 beat; AI server rack in a strong ramp-up with customer 
expansion and specification upgrades

Wistron’s July revenue was up 61% YoY to NT$308bn, or 30% higher than our estimate, 
reflecting strong AI server rack demand from neo-cloud and enterprises, offsetting the 
impact of the model transition. We expect sequential revenue growth ahead through 
Dec 2026, driven by new GPU AI server racks entering mass production in 4Q26, which 
will drive up the ASP , along with strong AI spending and the expansion of company’s 
customer base. The new GPU AI server racks have various racks across GPU, CPU, and 
LPU, supporting ODM growth in coming quarters. On PC, the company’s July shipments 
were at 1.6m units (-20% MoM / YoY), reflecting modest demand under high memory 
costs. Despite PC shipments likely being negative HoH considering the strong pull-in 
since late 2025, we expect the strong AI server rack ramp-up to offset the impact, 
driving 2H26 revenue up +28% HoH. Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wistron 
Our 12-month target price of NT$281 is based on a 13.1x 2027E P/E, which is derived 
from PC/server peer EPS growth vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a 
major factor for the stock’s share price performance.   
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected PC market recovery, 2) 
slower-than-expected AI server demand ramp-up, and 3) weaker-than-expected 
demand on general servers.

Gigabyte (2376.TW, Neutral): AI server in expansion; Strong capex commitment to capture the

rising AI server shipments, and we expect revenues to stay high in 3Q / 4Q26E post

remains positive on 2H26 growth driven by strong AI demand, customers’ faster AI 
deployment, and Gigabyte’s expanding customer base and enhancing services.

procurement to continue to improve in 2H26, supporting the procurement on AI servers.

ASUS (2357.TW, Neutral): PC enjoying product mix upgrade; AI server client base expansion; July &

Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger / weaker-than-expected PC market

### Page 11

Compal (2324.TW, Neutral): AI server capacity in expansion; July better than expected

Compal’s Jul revenue was up 38% YoY / down 15% MoM to NT$81bn, or 10% higher 
than our estimate, reflecting AI servers shipment ramp up. Jul PC shipments was down 
22% YoY / 25% MoM to 1.8m units, reflecting the impact of 1H26 demand pull-in amid 
the rising memory cost. We model sequential revenue growth in Aug-Dec, considering 
the company’s rising AI server shipments, AI server capacity expansion, and PC product 
mix upgrade towards high end model (e.g. AI PC). Overall, we expect Compal’s 3Q / 
4Q26E revenues to grow at 6% / 10% QoQ, benefiting from the rising AI server end 
demand from Neocloud clients. We remain positive on Compal’s business diversification, 
supported by global capacity expansion in the US, Taiwan, and Vietnam, while consumer 
electronics still accounts for the majority of the company’s total revenue, which demand 
could be capped by rising raw material costs (e.g., memory, CPU). Maintain Neutral on 
fair valuation.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Compal 
We are Neutral-rated on Compal with a 12-month target price of NT$41.3. Our target 
price is based on 13.1x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth and market re-rating for ODM 
companies as major factors for the stock’s performance.  
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger/weaker-than-expected demand for tablets.

### Page 13

Inventec (2356.TW, Neutral): AI server supporting growth ahead; Memory cost weighing on PC 
shipments; Jul Rev and 2Q26 beat

Inventec’s Jul revenue was up 66% YoY / down 12% MoM, or 32% higher than our 
estimate, which we attribute to the strong AI server end demand, while the MoM decline 
was mainly due to a decline in PC shipments, given the demand pull-in in 1H26. We 
expect sequential revenue decline in Aug-Sep, mainly on a decline in PC shipments from 
a consumption pull in amid rising memory costs. Meanwhile, growth momentum should 
recover in Oct-Dec, supported by AI server shipment ramp up. Overall, we expect the 
company to deliver double-digit YoY revenue growth in 3Q / 4Q26E, on rising AI server 
end demand and Inventec’s capacity expansion. We remain positive on Inventec’s AI 
server business expansion, while the rising memory cost would cap the PC shipments 
growth ahead. Maintain Neutral.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Inventec  
We are Neutral-rated on Inventec with a 12-month target price of NT$56.0. Our target 
price is based on 14.6x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

### Page 14

Pegatron (4938.TW, Sell): AI server driving 2H26 growth; PC growth capped by rising memory cost; Sell

Pegatron’s Jul revenue was +12% YoY / -6% MoM to NT$86bn, and we attribute the 
MoM decline to the sluggish PC end demand post the demand pull-in in 1H26, causing 
Pegatron’s PC shipments to decline to 0.40m units in Jul from 0.65m units in Jun. We 
model double digit MoM revenue growth in Aug-Sep, considering the low base in Jul, 
followed by modest sequential growth in Oct to Dec, mainly driven by the strong AI 
server shipments growth. Management remains positive on the momentum of AI server 
business in 2H26E, supported by the strong end demand from Neocloud and CSP clients. 
Overall, we expect Pegatron’s 3Q / 4Q26E revenues to grow at +20% / +27% YoY. We are 
positive on Pegatron’s AI server business expansion, while we expect the tight memory 
supply to cap the consumer electronics growth. Maintain Sell.

Earnings revision: Pegatron’s 2Q26 revenue, GM, and Opex ratio are largely in line with 
our estimate, while 2Q26 non-op income is higher than expected, leading 2Q26 net 
income 22% higher than our estimate. We factor in Pegatron’s Jul revenue and 2Q26 
result and raise our 2026-28E net incomes by 10% / 8% / 7%, mainly on higher revenues 
and lower opex ratios. We raise our 2026-28E revenues by 5% / 5% / 4%, mainly on 
higher contribution from AI server business. Our 2026-28E opex ratios are slightly 
revised up, mainly on higher R&D expense to support the AI server specification 
upgrades and PC product mix upgrade towards AI PC.

Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that
has passed a Phase II clinical trial nor a license to distribute a post-Phase II drug, treatment or medical device.  Rating Suspended (RS). Goldman Sachs
Research has suspended the investment rating and price target for this stock, because there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an
investment rating or target price. The previous investment rating and target price, if any, are no longer in effect for this stock and should not be relied

upon.  Coverage Suspended (CS). Goldman Sachs has suspended coverage of this company.  Not Covered (NC). Goldman Sachs does not cover this
company.

AirTAC International  | Asia Pacific
August Sales -7% MoM/+43%
YoY
Here we focus on AirTAC's monthly sales, which we believe
could be a catalyst for its share price.
What's new:
• August sales came in at NT$3,708mn (-7% MoM/+43% YoY), with the
sequential decline mainly due to seasonality.
• By segment, shipments of textiles (+47% YoY), electronics (+36% YoY),
machine tool (+35% YoY) and battery (+30% YoY) outperformed during the
month.
• QTD revenue reached 66% of our estimate at NT$11,586mn (-5% QoQ/+41%

YoY) and 70% of consensus' at NT$11,015mn (-9% QoQ/+34% YoY).
Outlook:
• We see its 3Q26 revenue  tracking in line with our estimate.
• Management remains positive on its growth outlook driven by its share gains
and the policy-supported industry upcycle, which it expects to extend into
2027.
• Stay OW, as current valuation of 20x 2027e P/E is undemanding vs. its mid-
cycle average of 25x.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AirTAC International (1590.TW)
Base case, 29x 2027e P/E. We believe this methodology better captures earnings growth
momentum for industrial automation component plays (market sentiment moves along
with business cycles). As the industry enters an up-cycle, we think applying a near-peak cycle
valuation of 29x P/E is reasonable given the stronger business outlook.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macroeconomic climate in China

n Stronger-than-expected demand from industrial automation
Risks to Downside
n Sharper economic downturn in China
n Miniature linear guideway business development taking more time
n Larger-than-expected investment in new products but weaker-than-expected demand

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Elite Material
Valuation
Our target price for EMC is set at NT$6,000, based on a target PE multiple of 30x on our 2027E EPS average. We believe our
target PE multiple, at the peak of its forward PE average in the past 5 years, is justified by a strong earnings outlook supported
by its leading position in CCL supply for AI GPU and ASIC (high m/s) from 2026E onwards. We also believe the continued spec

upgrade for CCL and price hikes should help support its margin expansion going forward. At our target price, the shares would
trade at 23.6x/13.2x 2026E/27E PB.
Risks
Citi’s quant system rates EMC high-risk given high share price volatility, which we largely attribute to frequent debates over the
spec upgrade for next-gen AI servers. In general, we believe some investors initially tend to have high hope for aggressive spec
upgrade without sufficiently considering the scalability and cost structure of CCL products, which then leads to a shortfall in

the end. However, from a longer-term perspective, we see a decent and clear spec upgrade trend with meaningful ASP growth
potential among AI servers of different generations. As such, we do not assign a high-risk rating.
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) a slower-than-expected CCL
upgrade trend; 2) weaker-than-expected AI server demand; 3) production bottlenecks in AI supply chain (eg, glass, copper foil,

foundry or OSAT); 4) unexpected share loss in key AI server projects; and 5) stricter practice of OOC policy by end customers.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

### Page 1

ab 6 September 2026
Global Research
Largan Precision
Strong CPO ramp priced ahead of qualification
The re-rating has outpaced qualification progress; downgrade from Buy to Sell
Largan shares have risen 242% since April on optimism around its CPO FAU progress.
While we stay constructive on Largan's CPO prospects, we believe the market is pricing
in confidence ahead of still-early qualification progress, particularly given a crowded
competitive field. Largan only starts its sample line late Q3, integrating its Prism Micro

Lens Array and Fiber Array with AOET's V-groove and Corning's fiber. We expect  several
quarters to improve yields, gain integrator acceptance (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and
convert its initial production-oriented FA program into volume while competing against
incumbent TFC and vertically integrated rivals incl. Coherent. We downgrade our rating
from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-
18x range well above our sum-of-the-parts NT$5,000 PT based on 15x smartphone lens

+ 50x CPO base case for 40% share  (upper end of optical peers  at 14-72x).
Still a range of outcomes for CPO solution traction
Largan's approach pairs V-groove with PMLA to shape the optical light from the PIC into
the fiber at high accuracy with automated assembly. Feedback is positive on Largan's
precision and track record for volume production/yield improvement (now still low) and
1 FA design in an FAU. A risk is Largan targeting the Gen 3 optics should take until 2028

and also competes with incumbent TFC which is already designed-in for 2026-27 and
other FAU vendors like Coherent that can vertically integrate or use meta lens not
requiring Largan's initial V-groove approach. Largan's push toward a complete solution
may also conflict with integration partners.
Core smartphone lens tracking behind amid memory headwinds
We also see risk on near-term results with Q326 sales tracking light. Aug sales were NT
$5bn, +3% MoM, -16% YoY, taking QTD revenue to NT$10.1bn, 53% of UBSe/Street

Q326. Sept would need to grow +73% MoM to meet UBSe/Street +36-38% QoQ vs.
normal seasonal +8% MoM. We expect flattish builds at best for its flagship customer
(80% of sales) needing to raise price to pass on memory costs +US$200-300 YoY albeit
some support from high-end introducing variable-aperture lens, while foldable lens
specs remain similar. Factoring muted smartphone growth but healthy margins, we view
a mid-teens multiple appropriate for this business, in line with its historical range.

Stock prices in our upside case ahead of traction; risk-reward skews negative
We maintain basic '26E/27E EPS at NT$190/NT$214 and NT$5,000 SOTP PT based on 1)
15x smartphone lens (NT$3,270) + 2) 50x CPO base case EPS of NT$35 for NT$1,730
(40% share, 100k switches/7.2mn FAUs, US$150/FAU, US$1.1bn TAM), high-end of
peers due to its premium ~40-45% OpM. We view risk/reward skewed negative with
downside to NT$2,500 on limited CPO success and softer smartphones, base case NT

$5,000 for CPO traction, and upside 22% if the stock is bid to our upside case NT$9,000
based on top-end 70x P/E on CPO earnings at 60% share of a higher US$1.58bn TAM.

### Page 2

Largan Precision 6 September 2026 ab 2
Largan Precision UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Could Largan's CPO products contribute meaningful revenue in a few years?
We stay constructive but the company still has hurdles to clear in a crowded space. Its scale and track
record in high-end smartphone lens gives it an opportunity supplying key components of the CPO

FAU assembly, traditional strength in precision lens micro assemblies, alignment accuracy, in-house
tooling and automation that could give it a differentiated solution. Our feedback at Semicon Taiwan
suggests the company is still early in qualification, with its sample production line for customer
evaluation readied in late September and still requiring work with a system integrator and
qualification with the fabless customer and ODMs.  We still see a year to move from sampling to

design-in but if successful timed to meet expected market acceleration in 2028-29.
Q: Will there be further technology upgrades driving the lens cycle in the coming years?
We expect spec upgrades for Largan's core lens business to come in H226 from variable aperture
shipments for the iPhone 18 (which Genius does not currently participate though may enter over
time, as seen with periscope lenses). Key offset though is weaker sales with Q3 tracking light and

suggesting a softer build due to memory headwinds. Further upside could come from broader
adoption of 48MP modules in future iPhone models. Looking longer term, Apple is reportedly
exploring 200MP periscope lens and adjacent opportunities to penetrate physical AI categories with
precision lens including robotics, autonomous systems and AR/VR devices.
We downgrade our rating on Largan from Buy to Sell as we view the market expectations as now
pricing in a bullish scenario for strong market traction with the company still at an early stage of

qualification, still needing to improve its process and yields, and competing in a crowded space also
against an existing incumbent. We expect several quarters to improve current low yields, gain
acceptance from solution integrators (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and secure design-ins by the AI GPU
fabless company and its ODM partners against incumbent TFC and other rivals. We downgrade
Largan from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-18x

range and well above our sum-of-the-parts NT$5,000 price target based on 15x smartphone lens +
50x CPO base case for40% share  (upper-end of optical peer range of 14-72x).
Largan has demonstrated its samples with partners at Computex and Semicon Taiwan and cited its
goals to ramp CPO as its next opportunity. The company is still early in qualifications with a sample
automated line in the coming months followed by design-in engagement with system integrators,

ODMs and the fabless customer to target design-in on 3rd gen CPO solutions in 2028. For
smartphone lens, its top customer had a strong iPhone 17 cycle although acknowledged it would
have to raise price to pass on higher memory costs which could dampen growth. Sales QTD are
tracking slightly light as Largan also competes with 2nd source Genius. Our supply chain checks and
management commentary points to introduction of higher value variable aperture lens in H226.

Largan is currently trading above the high end of its historical range at 39x/35x our 2026E/27E EPS vs.
a long-term range of 13-18x forward consensus earning, pricing in success penetrating the CPO
component market ahead of the earnings stream which would only start late in 2027 into 2028. We
note optical component peers trade at 14-72x with an average of ~38x 2027E P/E (Genius at 25x
2027 P/E). We maintain our SOTP valuation with our price target at NT$5000 based on 1) 15x

smartphone lens (NT$3,270) + 2) 50x CPO base case EPS of NT$35 for NT$1,730 (40% share, 100k
switches/7.2mn FAUs, US$150/FAU, US$1.1bn TAM), high-end of peers due to its premium ~40-
45% OpM. We view the stock at NT$7,400 now pricing in CPO traction on a 50x multiple of our
upside NT$76 case based on 60% share of an upside US$1.6bn opportunity.

### Page 3

Largan Precision 6 September 2026 ab 3
Largan Precision UBS Research
commentary
Largan shares have risen 242% since 1 April on optimism around its CPO fiber array unit
(FAU) progress. While we stay constructive post our feedback at Semicon Taiwan on
Largan's ability to develop CPO solutions, we now believe the market is pricing in a level
of confidence that has run well ahead of qualification progress which is still early and
competing in a crowded field. Largan will only start its sample line in late Q3 integrating

its Prism Micro Lens Array and Fiber Array, AOET's V-groove and Corning's glass fiber.
From there, we expect several quarters to improve current low yields, gain acceptance
from solution integrators (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and secure design-ins by the AI
GPU fabless company and its ODM partners against incumbent TFC and rivals including
Coherent with own lens/FAU. The company did note on its Q226 call that it had secured
one mass production commitment for its fiber array to an FAU maker that could ramp by

H227 although early to conclude the magnitude of this engagement or end customer
design-in, with the other customers still at proof-of-concept phase.
We downgrade our rating on Largan from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and
the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-18x range and well above our sum-of-the-parts NT
$5,000 price target based on 15x smartphone lens + 50x CPO base case for 40% share
(upper-end of optical component peers trading at a  range of 14-72x).

Still a range of outcomes for CPO solution traction
Largan's approach pairs V-groove with PMLA to shape the optical light from the PIC into
the fiber at high accuracy with automated assembly. Feedback is positive on Largan's
precision and track record for volume production/yield improvement (now still low). A
risk is Largan targeting the Gen 3 optics should take until 2028 and also competes with

incumbent TFC which is already designed-in for 2026-27 and other FAU vendors like
Coherent that can vertically integrate or use meta lens not requiring Largan's initial V-
groove approach. Largan's business model may also conflict with integration partners as
it also attempts to build toward a complete solution.
We estimate Largan's Total Addressable Market in a downside case is US$270mn if it
only supplies sub-components into a market that continues to push out CPO due to

reliability concerns relative to pluggables and NPO/XPO options, US$1.08bn in a base
case with 100k switches and 72 FAUs per switch at US$150 ASP , and US$1.6bn in an
upside case with 125k switches, 72 FAUs per switch and US$175 ASP .  For Largan, we
estimate downside case 10% share yields NT$810mn sales and NT$2.20 EPS, base case
40% share, NT$13.0bn sales and NT$35 EPS, and upside case 60% share, NT$28.4bn
sales and NT$76 EPS.
Although we are optimistic on the market opportunity scaling up and Largan's

competencies and progress developing these solutions, Largan's traction is not yet
certain and it will compete in a crowded landscape. We believe Himax is already
supplying wafer level optics for the collimator function with FOCI (3363.TWO) and TFC
(300394.SZ) already has demonstrated capability and design-in on the FAU and lens
assembly. Others that could vertically integrate include Coherent, Senko, Corning and
Sumitomo, and we also note Genius sending samples and Sunny Optical's intention to

develop for this market.

Largan Precision 6 September 2026 ab 4
Our feedback at Semicon Taiwan indicates Coherent can make the FAU and lens by itself
through capabilities merging with II-VI and demonstrated all in-house components at
the booth except for the metal transceiver casing.

Smartphone lens business tracking behind, should have a mild 2026-27
Largan reported August sales at NT$5bn,   is below seasonal 63-67% at only 53% of
Q326 UBSe/Street +36-38% QoQ and down 16% YoY, suggesting muted momentum
into its flagship customer (~80% of sales). July EPS was NT$13.94, 27% of UBSe Q3 NT
$51.52 suggesting in-line GM/OpM at ~50%/39% but the lighter sales are more of a

concern.
UBS expects flattish builds at best and a mild spec upgrade with one model variable
aperture but foldable at similar lens spec and higher memory cost (+US$200-300 YoY).
We still see mild growth and mid-teens multiple acceptable for the smartphone
business, similar to its long-term average.

Largn (3008.TW) trading at 7,400 at closed Sept 4.

UPSIDE (NT$9,000): Our upside valuation of NT$3,655 is based on  its average P/E of 16x
off our 2027 upside case EPS of NT$235. We factor in faster than expected
CPO progress to deliver upside EPS of NT$76 and 70x P/E at the high end of optical peers
trading at 14-72x 2027E P/E to an implied valuation of NT$5,345, and  total share price at
NT$9,000.
BASE (NT$5,000):  Our price target for its smartphone business is NT$3,270, with a 15x
2027 P/E applied on its base case EPS of NT$214 slightly below the long-term average of

its historical P/E band to reflect the macro uncertainty. Paired with our base scenario of its
CPO business at NT$35 EPS and 50x P/E, we arrive at an implied valuation of NT$1,730,
and Largan's aggregate PT  at NT$5,000.
DOWNSIDE (NT$2,500):   Downside valuation at NT$2,392, based on low-end of its
range 13x 2027 P/E. We take into consideration slower adoption of FAUs at its downside
EPS of NT$2 and the same 50x P/E to  an implied valuation of NT$108 for

CPO contribution, and Largan's total share price at NT$2,500.

Forecast returns
Forecast price appreciation -32.4%
Forecast dividend yield 1.1%
Forecast stock return -31.4%
Market return assumption 6.3%
Forecast excess return -37.6%
Company Description
Founded in 1987, Largan Precision (Largan) is one of the world's leading manufacturers of
lenses used in cameras and IT products. It specialises in plastic/hybrid lenses and, to a lesser
extent, glass lenses and modules. In 2022, 20MP+ camera lenses were 10-20% of Largan's

shipment mix, 10MP lenses were 50-60%, 8MP lenses were 0-10% and other lenses were
30-40%.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on SOTP valuation on the CPO opportunity and traditional lens
business.
We think Largan's downside risks include: 1) ongoing pricing pressure from smartphone OEM
clients and increasingly intense competition among lens suppliers; and 2) a slower-than-
expected smartphone demand recovery in China, and 3) CPO commercial success below the

market's elevated expectations.
We believe the key upside risk is a faster-than-expected CPO qualification process at Largan.
In addition, if the company demonstrates exceptionally efficient and highly accurate
automation capabilities, it could support greater market share gains within the industry than
we currently anticipate.

Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

公司簡介  
景碩科技股份有限公司創立於 2000 年 ， 為專
業載板全製程工廠 (Full Process Workshop) ，
提供客戶線路設計、光罩製作、基板生產到自
動電性檢測全製程服務 。其產品主要用途為半
導體構裝時作為晶片載體 ， 同時成為對外電路
連接的通道 ， 屬於封裝產業的原物料或者是載
體元件，銷售對象主要為國內外之 IC 封裝、
設計與系統業者， 主要生產基地位於台灣桃園
市新屋區、新竹縣新豐鄉、桃園市楊梅區及中
國江蘇省蘇州市。

營運展望 
1. 2Q26 需求強勁及 漲價效益 帶動營收獲利續成長 ：AI 需求帶動  ABF 與 BT 
進入結構型增長，以及漲價效益、需求強勁帶動第二季營收獲利大幅成長。景
碩第二季營收 125 億元、+12.5% QoQ、+30.7% YoY，毛利率 26.1%，營業利
益 17 億元、+88.5% QoQ、+170.8% YoY，營業利益率 13.4%。歸屬母公司稅
後淨利  13 億 元 ， 每 股 盈 餘 2.57 元 。 產 品 組 合 分 別 為 高 頻 環 氧 樹 脂 板 
(Ajinomoto Build -Up Fil m, ABF ) 45% (+27% YoY) 、 雙 馬 來 醯 亞 胺 樹 脂 板 
(Bismaleimide Triazine, BT) 38% (+39% YoY)、隱形眼鏡 (Lens) 17% (+24% YoY)。 
2. 高頻環氧樹脂板 (ABF) 稼動率維持高檔 ， 積極擴產滿足客戶需求：高速運算相
關 CPU、GPU、ASIC 等大晶片尺寸 應用仍然供不應求， 目前公司旗下三座  
ABF 工廠 (新屋廠、新豐廠、楊梅廠) 平均產能利用率約為 85%，隨 AI 需求
強勁增長及新專案陸續進場，預 期 2026 年底產能利用率將攀升至 90 ~ 95% 
之飽和區間。為因應未來幾年產能缺口，公司規劃於第六廠  (楊梅廠) 進行擴
產， 目前規劃分別為 2027 年擴增 25%、2028 年擴增 30%、2029 年擴增 23%。
儘管新產能開出時，因產能擴大將導致利用率短期下滑，但隨訂單持續挹注長
線利用率仍將朝 90 ~ 95% 之高檔水位靠攏。 
3. 雙馬來醯亞胺三嗪樹脂板 (BT) 受惠記憶體、光模組等強勁需求：因 AI 需求
強勁，記憶體與光模組 (Optical Module) 等客戶需求持續上修，目前 BT 平均
產能利用率約為 75%，預期 2026 年底產能利用率將攀升至 85%。既有產能已
能滿足記憶體和光模塊等客戶需求，故雙馬來醯亞胺樹脂板方面暫無擴產計畫。
傳統 電路板在進入 800G、1.6T 或 3.2T 世代後已無法滿足高頻細線路需求 ，
且以上規格生產過程 須導入載板及  mSAP 製程，業界 普遍共識光模組 已是 
2027 ~ 2029 年 BT 重要業務發展目標。 
4. 封裝載板全新上升循環，明後年新產能開出後營運獲利再上一階：受惠 AI 伺
服器與 HPC 需求，ABF 載板產能利用率近滿載並啟動擴產和漲價。同時 BT 
載板受惠記憶體與光模組市場強勁需求，也逐漸調漲報價反映成本。封裝載板
產業進入全新上升循環，預期下半年報價仍將逐季調漲。待明後年新產能陸續
開出後，營運將正式邁入爆發期 。合庫預估 2026 年營收 530 億元、+34.5% 
YoY，毛利率 26.4%。營業利益 71 億元、+167.2% YoY，營業利益率 13.5%。
歸屬母公司稅後淨利 53 億元，每股盈餘 10.25 元。 
5. ESG (景碩在 2026 年上市公司治理評鑑結果為 21 ~ 35%)：景碩除由永續經營
委員會提升跨部門協作以推動環境、社會與公司治理及氣候變遷因應之目標與
執行進度 ， 亦每季向董事會報告執行情形 ， 進一步強化內部組織治理精進作為 。
此外，公司亦強化供應鏈永續稽核作業，針對勞工權益、健康與安全及管理制
度等面向持續檢視與改善，致力建構永續合作網絡。

股價相對大盤走勢

大銀微系統 (4576 TT)  Buy

先進封裝、矽光子相關商機 2H26 持續擴大，評等為 Buy。 
投資建議 ：受惠於半導體前段製程、 CoWoS 測試設備相關訂單
挹注，以及矽光子檢測設備相關訂單數量自 07/2026 開始放大，
微米及奈米級定位系統 2H26 營收成長性可期，加上精密運動
及控制元件需求亦持續回溫，故群益的投資建議為 Buy 。  
2Q26 營業利益 QoQ+28.89% ，但受到匯兌損失影響，稅後淨利
QoQ-0.73% ：2Q26 營收 10.23 億元， QoQ+19.70% ，除精密運動
及控制元件營收動能續強外，微米及奈米級定位系統需求亦再
增溫，相關營收 5.34 億元， QoQ+17.20% ，雖然毛利率受到匯率
影響而降低至 37.10% ，但營業利益 QoQ+28.89% ，惟業外表現
不及 1Q26(1Q26 認列 0.10 億元匯兌利益， 2Q26 認列 0.37 億元
匯兌損失 )，稅後淨利 1.15 億元， QoQ-0.73% 。  
2H26 營收展望佳： 展望 2H26 ，微米及奈米級定位系統營收成
長性仍可期，目前訂單能見度達 5~6 個月，動能主要來自半導
體相關產品，原因有二， (1) 由於前段製程設備大廠訂單穩定挹
注，此部份商機主要來自台灣晶圓代工廠持續擴充先進製程，
(2) 台灣設備供應商 CoWoS 測試設備相關訂單數量持續增加，
且 CoPoS 、FOPLP 測試設備相關產品已送樣至客戶認證。此外，
矽光子檢測設備相關訂單自 04/2026 開始出貨給台灣半導體設
備供應商，且數量自 07/2026 開始放大， 07~08/2026 達數百台，
客戶並於 08/2026 兩度追加訂單，訂單能見度達 3~4 季。  
為因應微米及奈米級定位系統需求增溫，大銀微陸續啟動兩階
段擴產作業，第 1 階段已於 06/2026 完成，產能擴大約 20% ，
第 2 階段則預計於 2026 年年底至 2027 年年初之間完成，屆時
產能將再擴大 20% ，均為營收持續成長的後盾。  
至於精密運動及控制元件 2H26 營收展望亦佳，包括中國、東南
亞 (新加坡、印度等 )等地 PCB 鑽孔機、成型機設備供應商相關
需求自 4Q25 開始回溫，目前訂單能見度為 2.5~3 個月，營收成
長性較佳的產品包括線性馬達、伺服馬達、驅動器等。

2 群益投顧 
  企業 ESG 資訊揭露：  
就因應氣候變遷或溫室氣體管理之策略而言，大銀微成立 「氣候變遷風險小
組」，由董事長主持，主導全公司減碳因應方針及短、中、長期減碳策略，
依循氣候相關財務揭露建議書 (TCFD) 架構以進行氣候風險及機會評估，並
透過暴露程度與脆弱度矩陣研擬調適行動計畫，分別進行氣候變遷風險分
析評估潛在財務衝擊。  
再就企業溫室氣體排放量減量之預算與計畫而言，大銀微委託外部輔導團
隊協助教導 ISO 14064 溫室氣體盤查類別 3~類別 5 的執行項目，藉由盤查
間接溫室氣體所產生的碳排放數據，重整與規劃制定相關管理措施，使其降
低溫室氣體排放。至於大銀微持續進行節能減碳改善規劃方案則主要有五，
(1) 安裝節能智慧電表，結合節能 e 化系統管控電力使用，找出節能方案，
(2) 空壓機系統更換變頻空壓機或租賃馬力數較低的空氣用量， (3) 汰換冰水
機，選擇高效能的變頻冰水節能設備， (4) 分析找尋替代低碳及可回收材料，
並應用於產品循環經濟設計上， (5) 培養員工節能減碳的理念並養成節約用
電的好習慣。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (1 15)金管投顧新字第 016 號 
公司拜訪快報

宜鼎(5289) 
建議：維持買進 
結論：價值取勝策略搭配較佳客戶結構，長期獲利成長
性遠勝同業

一 、 宜鼎為工控記憶體模組大廠 ， 工業SSD 全球市占第
一 
宜鼎 100%營收皆來自於非消費性業務，以 InnoDisk 品
牌行銷全球 ， 工控SSD 全球市占第一 ， 並為全球前十大
DRAM 模組供應商，就產品結構而言，Flash、DRAM、
AI Solution 和其他分別占 1H26 營收比重為 32%、64%、
4%，因 Flash 產品客製化產品較高 ， 毛利率優於DRAM
產品。就營收區域而言，台灣、歐洲、美洲、中國、亞
洲 、 和其他分別占1H26 營收比重為 26%、14%、18%、
20%、21%和 1%。宜鼎於工控領域國內競爭對手包含
創見、宇瞻、威剛。因記憶體廠商競爭門檻在於料源取
得以及通路佈建 ， 宜鼎在料源部分與日美韓廠皆有合作 ，
且工控通路佈局綿密，客戶超過四千家顯現營收來源相
當分散。宜蘭二廠於 2024 年七月啟用，主要生產 AI 
Camera 和 AI 相關周邊，2026 年產能 1080K，最大產
能為 1440K。 
二、2Q26 EPS 108.93 元創新高並優於預期，3Q26 獲
利可望維持高檔  
2Q26 營收 224.4 億元(+70%QoQ)優於預期之 204.5 億
元，其中以 Flash 產品成長性較佳，毛利率 65%優於前
季之 59.1%，營業利益 131.46 億元(+92%QoQ)優於預
估值 21%，業外主因匯兌認列收益 0.69 億元，稅後純
益 103.68 億元(+90%QoQ)，EPS 108.93 元創新高並優
於預期之 89.95 元。展望 3Q26，客戶需求持續成長，
且料源獲得原廠支持，估計 3Q26 營收 258.6 億元
(+16%QoQ)， 因低價庫存利益降低 ， 毛利率 55%低於上
季 65%，稅後純益 102.1 億元(-2%QoQ)，EPS 107.27
元。 
三、產業供不應求將延續至 2027 年，公司為原廠產能
優先分配對象 
CSP 業者持續擴大投資 AI 相關基礎建設，集邦預估
2026 年和 2027 年全球九大 CSP 資本支出分別為 8,867
億美元(+90%YoY)和 1.32 兆美元(+49%YoY)，2026 年
AI 相關需求已經成為記憶體產業最大單一應用需求，主
要原廠亦將 AI 相關 Server 應用需求列為產能優先分配
對象，產業供不應求以及資源排擠效應，致使記憶體價
格自 4Q25-2Q26 連三季季漲幅超過 50%，預期產業供
不應求將延續至 2027 年。宜鼎客戶聚焦 AI 基礎建設、
全球垂直應用品牌以及高附加價值工控解決方案供應商
等三大類型，除客戶成本耐受能力高外，且此三類型客
戶亦為原廠產能優先支援對象，此為今年前七月營收
+549%YoY 成長性領先國內模組廠主因。 
四、1H26 AI 占比提升至 40%，高附加價值解決方案為
長期獲利成長來源 
AI 基礎建設已切入全球雲端服務供應商資料中心 、AI 加
速卡、前幾大光通訊與網路交換器客戶，Edge AI 趨勢
有助於記憶體搭載量提升，涵蓋人形機器人等物理 AI
以及企業級邊緣 AI 應用 ， 公司整合Storage、Memory、
Sensor、周邊系統、加速卡以及 AI Platform、韌體與管
理軟體給不同需求類型 Edge AI 客戶群，1H26 Edge AI
營收占比已達 40%遠優於先前預期之全年 30%目標 ， 故
公司獲利成長不僅僅是受惠於低價庫存，而是在於其 AI
拓展有成，以及其解決方案對於客戶之高附加價值。 
五、宜蘭三廠擴建預計 2028 年完成，既有產能將可支
應未來兩年成長  
為因應客戶長線需求，宜鼎將進行宜蘭三廠擴建，擴建
計畫包含地上八層以及地下兩層的三廠以及員工宿舍，
產能規模將超越目前宜蘭一廠以及二廠總和，4Q26 將
開始動工，並預計 2028 年完工投產，目前宜蘭二廠產
能預計仍可滿足未來兩年客戶需求，故不會有產能不足
問題。 
六、維持買進理由 
1)預估 2026 年和 2027 年 EPS 分別為 375.31 元和
388.33 元。 
2)考量價值取勝策略搭配較佳客戶結構，長期獲利成長
性遠勝同業，維持買進評等，TP 2100 元 (2027 PB x 
3)。

聯茂 (6213 TT) 
2H26 報價漲幅優於預期

永豐觀點 
聯茂受惠 E-Glass 供給吃緊，2Q26 產品報價優於預期帶動毛利率季增 10 個百分
點，2H26 漲價趨勢可望延續，單季漲幅達 15-20%，長線 M7 及 M9 料號可望於
2027 年貢獻，產品組合可望轉佳。 
 投資評價與建議 
受惠 E-Glass 供給吃緊 ， 聯茂M6 以下產品 2H26 報價逐季漲價 15-20%優於原先
預期 ， 漲價趨勢可望延續至1H27，2027 年在 Oak Stream 導入 M7 以及美系 GPU
客戶 M9 小量貢獻後 ，M7 以上高階料號占比由 2026 年的 2-3%跳升至 15-17%，
帶動產品組合轉佳，投資建議維持買進，目標價上調至 756 元(25X2027 EPS)。

ESG 評析 
聯茂企業永續評鑑整體為 B 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B 等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等
